Visualização: INTEGRAÇÃO FOLHA (Campos: 105)

Tipo de visualização: Local

Comando SQL:
SELECT Fpfunc.fu_depto, Fpevent.ev_prodes, Fpevent.ev_codigo, SUM(Fplanc.lc_valor) AS lc_valor, SUM(Fplanct.lt_tt_irrf) AS lt_tt_irrf, SUM(Fplanct.lt_tt_inss) AS lt_tt_inss, SUM(Fplanct.lt_tt_insd) AS lt_tt_insd, SUM(Fplanct.lt_tt_insp) AS lt_tt_insp, SUM(Fplanct.lt_tt_fgts) AS lt_tt_fgts, SUM(Fplanct.lt_tt_fa13) AS lt_tt_fa13 FROM dfp!fpfunc INNER JOIN dst!stpess ON Fpfunc.fu_pess = Stpess.pe_codigo INNER JOIN dfp!fpsalar ON Fpfunc.fu_codigo = Fpsalar.sa_func AND Fpsalar.sa_refer = ( ?System.ActiveDate ) INNER JOIN dfp!fplanc ON Fpfunc.fu_codigo = Fplanc.lc_func INNER JOIN dfp!fpevent ON Fpevent.ev_codigo = Fplanc.lc_event INNER JOIN dfp!fplanct ON Fplanc.lc_func = Fplanct.lt_func AND ( Fplanc.lc_mesano = Fplanct.lt_mesano AND Fplanc.lc_tipo = Fplanct.lt_tipo ) WHERE Fplanc.lc_mesano = ( ?System.ActiveReferency ) AND ( Fplanc.lc_tipo IN ("1","5") AND ( Fplanct.lt_tipo IN ("1","5") AND Fpfunc.fu_adm_dt <= ( ?System.ActiveDateEnd ) ) ) GROUP BY Fpfunc.fu_depto, Fpevent.ev_prodes, Fpevent.ev_codigo ORDER BY Fpfunc.fu_depto, Fpevent.ev_prodes, Fpevent.ev_codigo
